# ZXing's test images for the real-image tests
# (tools/fetch_real_images.sh).
# Each line: file name, SHA-256, URL, and what the file is: "png" for an
# image, which tools/png_to_bmp.py writes as a 24-bit BMP (the same
# pixels on every platform), or "text" for the text of the symbol in the
# image of the same name, which is kept as it is. "text+sjis" is such a
# text of which tools/text_to_sjis.py also writes the Shift JIS form,
# <name>.sjis, where it has one that is not the same bytes: the reader
# reports the bytes of a QR Code symbol as they are encoded.
#
# The test images of the ZXing project ("Zebra Crossing",
# https://github.com/zxing/zxing, Apache License 2.0), at commit
# 56b8a9a7b53392097891463023a26c406b422225: every image of the
# directories core/src/test/resources/blackbox/<series> of the
# symbologies the code reader reads, each with the text that ZXing's own
# tests expect of it (the .txt file of the same name, in UTF-8).
#
# The linear symbologies: 487 images in 23 series. zxing_<series>_<NN>
# is image <n> of the series; some series have gaps in their numbers.
#
# Data Matrix: 59 images in 3 series, at the end. zxing_<series>_<name>
# is the image <name>.png of the series. Not listed: the two
# <name>.metadata.txt files of datamatrix-1 (what ZXing's tests expect
# besides the text), and its
# HelloWorld_Text_L_Kaywa_6_error_byte.png.error, an image that the
# collection keeps out of its own tests by that name.
#
# QR Code: 179 images in 6 series, after those. zxing_<series>_<NN> is
# image <n> of the series; qrcode-2 has no image 3. Not listed: the
# three <n>.metadata.txt files of qrcode-1, qrcode-2 and qrcode-6 (what
# ZXing's tests expect besides the text).
#
# code128-1: 7 images
